### Atmospheric Dispersion and Transport

> **What?** The processes by which particles released into the atmosphere are moved (transported) by wind and spread out (dispersed) due to turbulent air motions and atmospheric conditions.

> **How?** These tools help model particle dispersion in the atmosphere to determine where particles will travel or initially originated from.

* [WAM2layers](https://github.com/WAM2layers/WAM2layers) ⭐ 73 | 🐛 34 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-07-07 - Used to determine where precipitation originally evaporated (backtracking), or where evaporated moisture eventually ends up (forward tracking).
* [MPTRAC](https://github.com/slcs-jsc/mptrac) ⭐ 55 | 🐛 11 | 🌐 C | 📅 2026-08-14 - A Lagrangian particle dispersion model for the analysis of atmospheric transport processes in the free troposphere and stratosphere.
* [CloudDrift](https://github.com/Cloud-Drift/clouddrift) ⭐ 46 | 🐛 62 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-07-22 - Accelerates the use of Lagrangian data for atmospheric, oceanic, and climate sciences.
* [IPART](https://github.com/ihesp/IPART) ⭐ 33 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2023-07-13 - A Python package for automated Atmospheric River (AR) detection, axis finding and AR tracking from gridded Integrated Vapor Transport data.
* [SNAP](https://github.com/metno/snap) ⭐ 28 | 🐛 10 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2026-08-13 - A lagrangian type atmospheric dispersion model specialized on modelling dispersion of radioactive debris.
* [GRAL](https://github.com/GralDispersionModel/GRAL) ⭐ 24 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 C# | 📅 2026-03-08 - A Lagrangian dispersion model with reasonable demands on computational times and sensible accuracy.
* [GasDispersion.jl](https://github.com/aefarrell/GasDispersion.jl) ⭐ 18 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Julia | 📅 2025-12-31 - A set of tools for atmospheric dispersion modeling of gaseous releases, such as might occur during an emergency at a chemical plant or more routinely from a stack.
* [Flexpart](https://gitlab.phaidra.org/flexpart/flexpart) - A Lagrangian transport and dispersion model suitable for the simulation of a large range of atmospheric transport processes.

### Atmospheric Chemistry and Aerosol

> **What?** The study of the chemical composition, reactions, and transformations of particles and aerosols in the atmosphere and their impact on air quality, climate, and living systems.

> **How?** These tools help simulate exchange processes, design atmospheric chemistry and aerosol studies, and understand site-specific and global aerosols.

### Radiative Transfer

> **What?** Energy transfer in the form of electromagnetic radiation that involves interactions such as absorption, emission, and scattering.

> **How?** These tools help model and solve for radiative transfer, including solar and thermal radiation, radiative fluxes, and general circulation models.

## Climate Change

> The long-term alteration of Earth's climate, including average weather patterns and global temperatures.

### Earth and Climate Modeling

> **What?** Methods for simulating the dynamics of Earth's climate system, including atmosphere, oceans, land, and ice, to understand past, present, and future environmental changes and their impacts.

> **How?** These tools enable the building, execution, and scientific interpretation of Earth and climate models.

### Climate Data Standards

> **What?** Established guidelines, protocols, and frameworks that ensure the accurate, consistent, and interoperable gathering, storage, documentation, comparing, and sharing of climate-related data.

> **How?** These tools facilitate projects to be climate data compliant, such as following the conventions of Climate and Forecast (CF) and the Unidata network Common Data Form (netCDF).

* [xarray](https://github.com/pydata/xarray) ⭐ 4,188 | 🐛 1,425 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-14 - An open source project and Python package that introduces labels in the form of dimensions, coordinates, and attributes on top of raw NumPy-like arrays, which allows for more intuitive, more concise, and less error-prone user experience.
* [Zarr](https://github.com/zarr-developers/zarr-python) ⭐ 2,037 | 🐛 502 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-14 -  Provides an efficient, scalable, and flexible way to store and access large, multi-dimensional arrays, the core data format used in climate models and observational datasets.
* [netcdf-python](https://github.com/Unidata/netcdf4-python) ⭐ 835 | 🐛 159 | 🌐 Cython | 📅 2026-07-27 - The Unidata network Common Data Form (netCDF) in Python is an interface for scientific data access and a set of freely-distributed software libraries that provide an implementation of the interface.
* [icechunk](https://github.com/earth-mover/icechunk) ⭐ 657 | 🐛 271 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-08-11 - An open-source transactional storage engine for tensor / ND-array data designed for use on cloud object storage.
* [netcdf-c](https://github.com/Unidata/netcdf-c) ⭐ 600 | 🐛 296 | 🌐 C | 📅 2026-07-31 - The Unidata network Common Data Form (netCDF) in C is an interface for scientific data access and a set of freely-distributed software libraries that provide an implementation of the interface.
* [kerchunk](https://github.com/fsspec/kerchunk) ⭐ 366 | 🐛 130 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-03-30 - A library that provides a unified way to represent a variety of chunked, compressed data formats (e.g. NetCDF, HDF5, GRIB), allowing efficient access to the data from traditional file systems or cloud object storage.
* [netcdf-fortran](https://github.com/Unidata/netcdf-fortran) ⭐ 270 | 🐛 124 | 🌐 Fortran | 📅 2026-07-29 - The Unidata network Common Data Form (netCDF) in Fortran is an interface for scientific data access and a set of freely-distributed software libraries that provide an implementation of the interface.
* [h5netcdf](https://github.com/h5netcdf/h5netcdf) ⭐ 227 | 🐛 23 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-07-20 - A Python interface for the netCDF4 file format that reads and writes local or remote HDF5 files directly via h5py or h5pyd, without relying on the Unidata netCDF library.
* [NCDatasets.jl](https://github.com/JuliaGeo/NCDatasets.jl) ⭐ 191 | 🐛 27 | 🌐 Julia | 📅 2026-07-31 - Allows one to read and create netCDF files, with data sets and attribute lists behaving like Julia dictionaries and variables like Julia arrays, and it implements the CommonDataModel.jl interface.
* [cf-xarray](https://github.com/xarray-contrib/cf-xarray) ⭐ 180 | 🐛 88 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-01 - An accessor for xarray objects that interprets CF attributes.
* [cf-python](https://github.com/NCAS-CMS/cf-python) ⭐ 151 | 🐛 131 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-14 - A CF-compliant Earth Science data analysis library.
* [IOOS Compliance Checker](https://github.com/ioos/compliance-checker) ⭐ 131 | 🐛 42 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-13 - A python based tool for data providers to check for completeness and community standard compliance of local or remote netCDF files against CF and ACDD file standards.
* [cf-conventions](https://github.com/cf-convention/cf-conventions) ⭐ 100 | 🐛 20 | 🌐 R | 📅 2026-07-30 - The conventions for CF (Climate and Forecast) metadata are designed to promote the processing and sharing of files created with the NetCDF API.
* [CF Checker](https://github.com/cedadev/cf-checker) ⭐ 81 | 🐛 33 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2024-08-02 - The CF Checker is a utility that checks the contents of a NetCDF file complies with the Climate and Forecasts (CF) Metadata Convention.
* [Climate Model Output Rewriter](https://github.com/PCMDI/cmor) ⭐ 61 | 🐛 14 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2026-08-14 - Produce CF-compliant netCDF files that fulfill the requirements of many of the climate community's standard model experiments.
* [cfdm](https://github.com/NCAS-CMS/cfdm) ⭐ 31 | 🐛 43 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-14 - Fully implements the CF data model for its internal data structures and so is able to process any CF-compliant dataset.
* [pycmor](https://github.com/esm-tools/pycmor) ⭐ 11 | 🐛 45 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-09 - A Python package to simplify the standardization of output into the Climate Model Output Rewriter standard.

### Climate Data Processing and Analysis

> **How?** These tools provide processing and data preparation toolkits for different climate data formats, such as for geospatial analysis and hydrological modeling.

### Climate Downscaling

> **What?** The process of transforming the output of global climate models into higher-resolution, fine-scale climate information to better represent local climate variations and support regional assessments and decision-making.

> **How?** These tools help perform climate downscaling through a diverse set of methods from statistics to deep learning.

* [cmip6-downscaling](https://github.com/carbonplan/cmip6-downscaling) ⭐ 213 | 🐛 34 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2026-06-24 - Climate downscaling using CMIP6 data.
* [scikit-downscale](https://github.com/pangeo-data/scikit-downscale) ⭐ 196 | 🐛 13 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-07-06 - Statistical downscaling and postprocessing models for climate and weather model simulations.
* [downscaleCMIP6](https://github.com/ClimateImpactLab/downscaleCMIP6) ⭐ 153 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2025-05-28 - Global Downscaled Projections for Climate Impacts Research.
* [climate4R](https://github.com/SantanderMetGroup/climate4R) ⭐ 143 | 🐛 19 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2025-11-05 - A bundle of R packages for transparent climate data access, post-processing (including data collocation and bias correction / downscaling) and visualization.

### Natural Hazard and Storms

> **What?** Naturally occurring environmental events that pose a potential threat to a community or society include storms, which are disturbed atmospheric states characterized by violent weather, including strong winds, heavy precipitation, thunder, and lightning.

> **How?** These tools forecast, simulate, and estimate the impact from natural hazards, such as floods, hurricanes, and tropical cyclones, and help design risk reduction strategies.

### Integrated Assessment and Climate Policy

> **What?** Frameworks that draw from multiple disciplines, such as economics, psychology, climate science, and policy, to analyze and project how human activities and policies affect and interact with the environment and climate.

> **How?** These tools help perform integrated assessment models and scenario analysis; collect, study, and forecast the effects of climate policies; and model broader societal behaviors related to the interactions between society and the environment.

* [GCAM](https://github.com/JGCRI/gcam-core) ⭐ 424 | 🐛 274 | 🌐 R | 📅 2026-06-01 - A dynamic-recursive model with technology-rich representations of the economy, energy sector, land use and water linked to a climate model that can be used to explore climate change mitigation policies including carbon taxes, carbon trading, regulations and accelerated deployment of energy technology.
* [pyam](https://github.com/IAMconsortium/pyam) ⭐ 289 | 🐛 103 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-08 - A Python package for data-wrangling, analysis and visualization of integrated-assessment scenarios and energy systems modeling results.
* [MESSAGEix](https://github.com/iiasa/message_ix) ⭐ 150 | 🐛 143 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2026-08-11 - A versatile, dynamic systems-optimization modeling framework developed by the IIASA Energy, Climate, and Environment (ECE) Program since the 1980s.
* [LPJmL](https://github.com/PIK-LPJmL/LPJmL) ⭐ 97 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 C | 📅 2026-06-12 - Designed to simulate the global terrestrial carbon cycle and the response of carbon and vegetation patterns under climate change.
* [SDEverywhere](https://github.com/climateinteractive/SDEverywhere) ⭐ 83 | 🐛 39 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-08-14 - The framework for system dynamics models created for the global climate simulator En-ROADS, the climate policy simulator C-ROADS and the Energy Policy Simulator.
* [WorldDynamics.jl](https://github.com/worlddynamics/WorldDynamics.jl) ⭐ 75 | 🐛 24 | 🌐 Julia | 📅 2024-05-25 - Aims to provide a modern framework to investigate integrated assessment models of sustainable development, based on current software engineering and scientific machine learning techniques.

* [GCAM-China](https://github.com/umd-cgs/gcam-china) ⭐ 66 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 R | 📅 2026-04-09 - The Global Change Analysis Model with China provincial-level details.

* [nomenclature](https://github.com/IAMconsortium/nomenclature) ⭐ 24 | 🐛 50 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-14 - Allows managing definitions of data structures for model comparison projects and scenario analysis studies using the data format developed by the Integrated Assessment Modeling Consortium.
* [Future Technology Transformation](https://github.com/cpmodel/FTT_StandAlone) ⭐ 21 | 🐛 31 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-12 - Integrated assessment model with a realistic treatment of technology diffusion.

## Natural Resources

> Resources that stem from nature, including minerals, water, soil, forests, and fossil fuels.

### Air Quality

> **What?** A measure of the cleanliness of the air we breathe,  determined by the presence and concentration of pollutants such as particulate matter, ozone, nitrogen oxides, carbon monoxide, sulfur oxides, and lead.

> **How?** These tools implement air quality models to simulate and forecast the creation and dispersion of air pollution; gather, visualize, and make actionable air quality data; and provide firmware for air quality sensors.

### Soil and Land

> **What?** The uppermost layer of mineral and organic material that supports plants and ecosystems (soil), along with the terrestrial surface area that includes natural and modified landscapes (land).

> **How?** These tools help predict and map soil and land cover metrics, including moisture, health, respiration, and carbon stocks; gather and visualize soil and land data; and perform change prediction and detection.

### Ecotoxicology

> **What?** The study of the toxic effects of chemicals and pollutants on living organisms within ecosystems.

> **How?** These tools help assess and map the human health risks from environmental exposures, including chemicals and other toxins.

* [toxpiR](https://github.com/ToxPi/toxpiR) ⭐ 11 | 🐛 7 | 🌐 R | 📅 2026-06-15 - R package for the Toxicological Priority Index (ToxPi) prioritization algorithm.

* [CLASSIC](https://gitlab.com/cccma/classic) - The Canadian Land Surface Scheme Including Biogeochemical Cycles is the land surface component of the CanESM.

## Sustainable Development

> Development that meets the needs of the present generation without compromising the ability of future generations to meet their own.

### Sustainable Development Goals

> **What?** 17 goals and 169 targets set by the United Nations to serve as a universal call to action to end poverty, protect the planet, and ensure that by 2030 all people enjoy peace and prosperity.

> **How?** These tools help collect, monitor, and track progress towards the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).

* [SustainBench](https://github.com/sustainlab-group/sustainbench) ⭐ 123 | 🐛 10 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2022-08-30 - Benchmarks for Monitoring the Sustainable Development Goals with Machine Learning.

* [OSDG](https://github.com/osdg-ai/osdg-tool) ⭐ 47 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2023-02-02 -  Tool that assigns Sustainable Development Goals labels to your input.
* [US Sustainable Development Goal indicators](https://github.com/GSA/sdg-indicators-usa) ⚠️ Archived - U.S. National Reporting Platform for the Sustainable Development Goals.
* [OSDG Community Dataset](https://github.com/osdg-ai/osdg-data) ⭐ 38 | 🐛 1 | 📅 2023-10-02 - A public dataset of thousands of text excerpts, validated by OSDG Community Platform citizen scientists with respect to the Sustainable Development Goals.

* [The SDG Transformation Center](https://sdgtransformationcenter.org/) - Science-based tools and analytics for SDG pathways, policies and financing.
* [Europe Sustainable Development Reports](https://eu-dashboards.sdgindex.org/) - Track the progress of the European Union and European Countries towards the Sustainable Development Goals.

### Population and Poverty

> **What?** Population is the total number of people living in a specific area. Poverty is a condition in which individuals lack the financial resources and essentials required for a basic standard of living.

> **How?** These tools facilitate access to population and census data; provide insight into upward mobility; and calculate poverty and inequality indices.

### Sustainable Investment

> **What?** An investment strategy that considers both financial and environmental goals.

> **How?** These tools help quantify the economic value of natural resources for decision-making support; assess the climate alignment and impact of financial portfolios; map the climate finance landscape; and explore innovative ways to fund conservation and restoration projects.

* [U.S. Climate Resilience Toolkit](https://toolkit.climate.gov/) - A website designed to help people find and use tools, information, and subject matter expertise to build climate resilience.
* [Climate Change Tracker](https://climatechangetracker.org/) - Empower individuals and communities to take action against the global threat of climate change by providing a reliable, user-friendly platform for tracking and understanding its progression.
* [NATUR'ADAPT](https://naturadapt.com/) - A community interested in climate change and its impacts on nature, especially in protected natural areas.

### Data Catalogs and Interfaces

> **What?** Centralized digital repositories and tools that organize, manage, and provide access to diverse environmental and climate-related datasets.

> **How?** These tools and repositories facilitate access to environmental and climate-related datasets and information.

* [Earthdata Search](https://github.com/nasa/earthdata-search) ⭐ 823 | 🐛 18 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-08-13 - A web application developed by NASA EOSDIS to enable data discovery, search, comparison, visualization, and access across EOSDIS' Earth Science data holdings.
* [Custom Scripts Sentinel Hub](https://github.com/sentinel-hub/custom-scripts) ⭐ 700 | 🐛 13 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-08-13 - A repository of custom scripts to be used with Sentinel Hub.
* [AI for Earth Data Sets](https://github.com/microsoft/AIforEarthDataSets) ⭐ 315 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2024-12-17 - Notebooks and documentation for AI-for-Earth-managed datasets on Azure.
* [eurostat R package](https://github.com/rOpenGov/eurostat) ⭐ 254 | 🐛 34 | 🌐 R | 📅 2026-07-17 - R tools to access open data from Eurostat, the statistical office of the European Union.
* [Digital Earth Africa Notebooks](https://github.com/digitalearthafrica/deafrica-sandbox-notebooks) ⭐ 238 | 🐛 9 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2026-08-07 - Using Earth observations to address social, environmental and economic changes on the Africa continent.
* [owidR](https://github.com/piersyork/owidR) ⭐ 125 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 R | 📅 2025-04-06 - An R Package for Interacting with Data from Our World in Data.
* [giscoR](https://github.com/rOpenGov/giscoR) ⭐ 80 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 R | 📅 2026-08-14 - An R API package that helps to retrieve data from Eurostat Geographic Information System of the Commission.
* [Resource Watch](https://github.com/resource-watch/resource-watch) ⭐ 73 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2024-05-07 - Features hundreds of data sets all in one place on the state of the planet's resources and citizens.
* [Planetary Computer Data Catalog](https://github.com/microsoft/PlanetaryComputerDataCatalog) ⭐ 45 | 🐛 30 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-06-26 - Combines a multi-petabyte catalog of global environmental data with intuitive APIs and a flexible scientific environment.
* [Climate Action Plans](https://github.com/mysociety/caps) ⭐ 12 | 🐛 83 | 🌐 HTML | 📅 2026-08-12 - A simple, open database of local government climate action plan documents and emissions data.
* [STAC Index](https://github.com/stac-utils/stac-index) ⭐ 10 | 🐛 15 | 🌐 Vue | 📅 2026-06-27 - Find STAC Catalogs, Collections, APIs, Software and Tools.
* [EEA geospatial data catalogue](https://github.com/eea/geonetwork-eea) ⭐ 8 | 🐛 10 | 🌐 Java | 📅 2026-07-15 - Discover and access easily the geospatial data catalogue of the European Environment Agency.
* [Climate Data Store](https://cds.climate.copernicus.eu/) - Dive into this wealth of information about the Earth's past, present and future climate.
* [Global Fishing Watch](https://globalfishingwatch.org/) - We use cutting-edge technology to visualise, track and share data about global fishing activity in near real-time and for free.
* [Global Wind Atlas](https://globalwindatlas.info/) - Immediately start exploring windy areas.
* [Global Solar Atlas](https://globalsolaratlas.info/) - Start exploring solar potential.
* [Food and Agriculture Organization Map Catalog](https://data.apps.fao.org/) - Allows to easily share geographically referenced thematic information between different organizations.
* [The Subak Data Catalogue](https://data.subak.org/) - Exists to make climate data more discoverable, more trusted and more connected.
* [Radiant MLHub Python Client](https://github.com/radiantearth/radiant-mlhub) - Open community commons for geospatial training data, machine learning models, and standards to encourage collaboration and share information.
* [Global Environmental Database](https://db.cger.nies.go.jp/portal/geds/index) - Providing long-term monitoring data, data analysis results, output of models.
* [EarthData](https://www.earthdata.nasa.gov/) - Our vision is to make NASA's free and open Earth science data interactive, interoperable, and accessible for research and societal benefit both today and tomorrow.
* [MeCCO](http://sciencepolicy.colorado.edu/icecaps/research/media_coverage/) - Open Database on the Media Coverage of Climate Change and Global Warming.
* [The POWER Project](https://power.larc.nasa.gov/) - Provides solar and meteorological data sets from NASA research for support of renewable energy, building energy efficiency and agricultural needs.
* [Global Energy Monitor](https://globalenergymonitor.org/) - Studies the evolving international energy landscape, creating databases, reports, and interactive tools that enhance understanding.
* [Eumap](https://gitlab.com/geoharmonizer_inea/eumap) - Comprises environmental, land cover, terrain, climatic, soil and vegetation layers covering the continental Europe at relatively fine spatial resolutions.
